bookmarksString) {\n        /* Format is {{ID}}q{{TIME}}-{{ID}}q{{TIME}}... */\n\n        ArrayList<Bookmark> bookmarks = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        if (bookmarksString == null || bookmarksString.isEmpty()) {\n            return bookmarks;\n        }\n\n        String[] pairs = bookmarksString.split("-");\n        for (String pair : pairs) {\n            Bookmark b = new Bookmark();\n            String[] info = pair.split("q");\n\n            if (info.length == 2) {\n                b.id = Integer.parseInt(info[0]);\n                b.created = Long.parseLong(info[1]);\n                bookmarks.add(b);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (sorted) {\n            Collections.sort(bookmarks, (b1, b2) -> Long.compare(b2.created, b1.created));\n        }\n\n        return bookmarks;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isBookmarked(Context ctx, int id) {\n        ArrayList<Bookmark> bookmarks = loadBookmarks(ctx, false);\n getTimeAgo(long time) {\n        if (time < 1000000000000L) {\n            // if timestamp given in seconds, convert to millis\n            time *= 1000;\n        }\n\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (time > now || time <= 0) {\n            return "?";\n        }\n\n        final long diff = now - time;\n        if (diff < MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "just now";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 min";\n        } else if (diff < 50 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / MINUTE_MILLIS + " mins";\n        } else if (diff < 120 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 hr";\n        } else if (diff < 24 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / HOUR_MILLIS + " hrs";\n        } else if (diff < 48 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 day";\n        } else if (diff < 365 * DAY_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / DAY_MILLIS + " days";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * YEAR_MILLIS) {\n url\n            try {\n                Intent browserIntent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(URLUtil.guessUrl(url)));\n                ctx.startActivity(browserIntent);\n            } catch getTimeAgo(long time) {\n        if (time < 1000000000000L) {\n            // if timestamp given in seconds, convert to millis\n            time *= 1000;\n        }\n\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (time > now || time <= 0) {\n            return "?";\n        }\n\n        final long diff = now - time;\n        if (diff < MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "just now";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 min";\n        } else if (diff < 50 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / MINUTE_MILLIS + " mins";\n * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 day";\n        } else if (diff < 365 * DAY_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / DAY_MILLIS + " days";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * YEAR_MILLIS) {\n Selecting and copying therefore produced a broken, incomplete link — exactly the reported bug. Truncation in the normal comment list is intentional (it keeps rows compact), so the fix belongs only on the select-text path.\n\n## Fix\n\n**`utils/Utils.java`** — added `expandTruncatedLinks(String html)`, which walks every `<a>` tag and replaces the link text with the full `href` when that text has been truncated:\n\n- The href is reinserted **still HTML-escaped**, so entities like `&#x2F;` and `&amp;` decode identically to before and the displayed URL is correct.\n- It only expands when the visible text (after entity decoding) is genuinely the *beginning* of the href — compared with the scheme stripped from both.
